106:     private void applyV300ConfigMigration() {
107:         int version = getConfig().getInt("config-version", 0);
108:         if (version >= 300) return;
109:         getConfig().set("config-version", 300);
110:         getConfig().set("base-hit.sword", 38.0);
111:         getConfig().set("base-hit.trident", 44.0);
112:         getConfig().set("abyssal-step.damage", 650.0);
113:         getConfig().set("tidal-crescent.damage", 650.0);
114:         getConfig().set("soul-undertow.damage", 650.0);
115:         getConfig().set("soul-undertow.swirl-force", 0.36);
116:         getConfig().set("combo.soul-break-bonus-damage", 42.0);
117:         getConfig().set("ultimate.pulse-damage", 165.0);
118:         getConfig().set("ultimate.collapse-damage", 900.0);
--- lines 133-158 ---
133:         getConfig().set("leviathan.leviathan-fang.damage", 650.0);
134:         getConfig().set("leviathan.maelstrom-prison.pulse-damage", 165.0);
135:         getConfig().set("leviathan.ultimate.charge-damage", 650.0);
136:         getConfig().set("leviathan.ultimate.collapse-damage", 1000.0);
137:         saveConfig();
138:         getConfig().set("gacha.cooldowns.skill-1", 10);
139:         getConfig().set("gacha.cooldowns.skill-2", 16);
140:         getConfig().set("gacha.cooldowns.skill-3", 24);
141:         getConfig().set("armor.cooldowns.phoenix", 45);
142:         getConfig().set("armor.cooldowns.voidwalker", 32);
143:         getConfig().set("armor.cooldowns.titan", 40);
144:         getConfig().set("armor.cooldowns.celestial", 38);
145:         getLogger().info("Migrated LegendaryRais config to v3.0.0 Legendary Arsenal defaults.");
146:     }

330:     private boolean matchesLegendaryWeapon(ItemStack item, Material material, String weaponId, int modelData,
331:                                             String itemModelKey, boolean loyalty) {
332:         if (item == null || item.getType() != material) return false;
333:         ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();
334:         if (meta == null) return false;
335: 
336:         String id = meta.getPersistentDataContainer().get(weaponKey, PersistentDataType.STRING);
337:         if (weaponId.equals(id)) {
338:             ensureLegendaryRuntimeMeta(item, weaponId, modelData, itemModelKey, loyalty);
339:             return true;
340:         }
341: 
342:         // Migration / self-heal path for weapons created by older LegendaryRais builds.
343:         // Recognise either the modern item_model, the old Soul Tide Katana model, or legacy CMD.
344:         String currentModel = readItemModel(meta);
--- lines 335-360 ---
335: 
336:         String id = meta.getPersistentDataContainer().get(weaponKey, PersistentDataType.STRING);
337:         if (weaponId.equals(id)) {
338:             ensureLegendaryRuntimeMeta(item, weaponId, modelData, itemModelKey, loyalty);
339:             return true;
340:         }
341: 
342:         // Migration / self-heal path for weapons created by older LegendaryRais builds.
343:         // Recognise either the modern item_model, the old Soul Tide Katana model, or legacy CMD.
344:         String currentModel = readItemModel(meta);
345:         boolean oldSoulModel = WEAPON_SOUL_TIDE.equals(weaponId) && "soultide:soul_tide_katana".equals(currentModel);
346:         if (itemModelKey.equals(currentModel) || oldSoulModel || modelData == readCustomModelNumber(meta)) {
347:             meta.getPersistentDataContainer().set(weaponKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, weaponId);
348:             String[] key = itemModelKey.split(":", 2);

177:     private List<LivingEntity> targets(Player p,double r,int max,boolean los){List<LivingEntity> out=new ArrayList<>();for(Entity e:p.getWorld().getNearbyEntities(p.getLocation(),r,r,r)){if(!(e instanceof LivingEntity l)||l.getUniqueId().equals(p.getUniqueId())||!l.isValid()||l.isDead())continue;if(los&&!p.hasLineOfSight(l))continue;out.add(l);}out.sort(Comparator.comparingDouble(x->x.getLocation().distanceSquared(p.getLocation())));return out.size()>max?new ArrayList<>(out.subList(0,max)):out;}
178:     private LivingEntity nearest(Player p,double r){List<LivingEntity> l=targets(p,r,1,true);return l.isEmpty()?null:l.get(0);}
